Never reuse excluded-paper outputs against changed source bytes.

## 6. Silver, scoring, and corrections

### 6.1 Silver and readiness

Silver stores common source/task/sample records and findings tied to immutable original packages. Accepted representations remain separate. It contains no synthesized extraction, canonical family graph or organization score. Every acceptance resolves to original objects and evidence.

A frozen receipt requires the complete original input pool; common source, policy and task revisions; terminal verdicts for all applicable selected tasks of active scored pipelines; resolved pointers/hashes/requirements/shards/weights; authorized witnesses for failures; complete searches for omissions; completed independent-review routes; no unresolved target defect or unanchored bucket; coherent findings within each reviewed representation; and reproducible gates, fractions and pair rows. Different admissible organizations across pipelines are allowed. Record which checks were mechanically exhaustive and which were scientifically sampled.

When findings conflict, reopen the joint case against original evidence. Do not choose convenient fields from different judgments or pipelines. If a common target changes, apply it to all affected outputs. Unresolved contradiction at the bound holds the paper.

### 6.2 Exact score

For pipeline m on eligible paper p, use:

\[
Q_{mp}=G_{mp}P_{mp}\frac{7a_{pC}C_{mp}+2a_{pT}T_{mp}}{7a_{pC}+2a_{pT}}.
\]

G is the mandatory package/mechanical gate. A content card receives bit 1 only when required information is present and every applicable scientific dimension passes; otherwise bit 0. Multiple shards pass only when all pass coherently; the card bit is their minimum. Unresolved cases have no bit and hold the paper. Average card bits within each sampled source tile.

`C_prose` is the mean of sampled prose/equation tile values. `C_table` is the mean across sampled source tables of each table's mean sampled content-block value. With both source strata present, `C=(C_prose+C_table)/2`; with one, use that stratum alone. Census/reallocated draws do not change these target weights. `aC=1` iff the verified common frame contains eligible content.

An archive block receives bit 1 only when all sampled source rows/cells and required headings, caption, annotations, panel identities and scopes are faithfully reconstructable; otherwise bit 0. Unsupported extra data in selected rows or mis-scoped notes also fail. T averages sampled block bits within each selected source table, then averages those table means. `aT=1` iff the source contains a qualifying numerical table. Empty miner tables never make T inapplicable.

T link conformance checks defined ordinary results and claimed uses. Missing scientific result content belongs to C; do not invent an omitted R object to cause a second T-link failure. A display-only archival precision loss, with unchanged scientific meaning/provenance, fails T alone. Incorrect units, row attachment or citations affecting a structured assertion are also assessed in that assertion's C/P case.

P is the mean of the selected occupied-source-bucket bits; each bucket requires every owned assertion/shard to pass. P=1 for an empty assertion set. A verified unsupported/untraceable assertion or demonstrably false provenance in the mandatory unanchored review sets P=0. A provenance failure in an ordinary selected source bucket contributes that bucket's zero to the mean. Missing required information still fails C. A root defect can fail both C and P through this formula; add no further field/link/count penalty. Repeated output views, shared objects and equivalent pipelines do not divide credit or create extra scoring mass.

Use exact reduced fractions throughout. Serialize integer numerator and positive denominator, converting Q once to finite `[0,1]` float for the competition adapter. G=0 gives Q=0; skipped components are null with `GATE_FAILED`, not measured zeros. Inapplicable components are null. If aC=aT=0, mark the paper INELIGIBLE without evaluating a denominator. All applicability flags and C/T weights are common across pipelines.

Required arithmetic examples: all checks pass gives 1; one of six equally weighted C tiles fails with T=P=G=1 gives 47/54; one of four sampled P buckets fails with C=T=G=1 gives 3/4; one failure in a census of two occupied P buckets with C=T=G=1 gives 1/2; C=P=1/2 and T=G=1 gives 11/36; no tables with C=5/6 and P=G=1 gives 5/6. Source/evaluator HOLD never becomes numeric Q. Complete fixture outcomes are in Appendix D.

### 6.3 Ranking and incumbent fallback

Give eligible papers equal weight. For each challenger compute `d_p=Q_challenger,p−Q_incumbent,p` on the same ordered eligible grid, mean gain, and sample-standard-deviation of gains divided by `sqrt(n)`. SE is infinite for n<2. Pipeline execution/gate failures remain zero; common exclusions are skipped for everyone. Sampled cases within a paper are not independent ranking observations.

Week-one challengers clear when mean gain is positive. With an incumbent, require n≥2 and mean gain greater than `2.13×SE`, subject to the required audits. Incumbent-team version swaps use nonnegative mean gain. Rank eligible finalists by mean gain; those within one paired SE of the leader tie for selection, with earliest commitment then hotkey lexical order winning. The paired SE between finalists uses per-paper differences of their gains, not added independent SEs. Preserve the existing entrant-count stage scheduling and mainnet absolute stage minima: prescreen 20, screen 120, final 500, launch/week-one 320. Before intake, freeze the planned sample size, an exact minimum-eligible fraction and absolute stage minima in the competition policy. For `claims_common_eligibility_v1`, require `max(ceil(planned_papers × numerator / denominator), absolute_stage_minimum)` eligible papers; the owner-approved starting fraction is 1/2. A custom testnet smoke evaluation may declare smaller absolute minima (at least one), so ten planned testnet papers can require five. Historical policies without this extension retain their original full-sample requirement. Include the policy in runtime hashing; never lower it after inspecting results.

For incumbent execution-failure fallback, exclude common-fault papers and require one terminal incumbent result per eligible paper. Trigger only when `5×failed_papers > eligible_papers`; 4/20 does not trigger, 5/20 does. Count build_failed, package_failed, invalid_output, timeout, cap_exceeded and security_violation. A delivered G=0 package contributes derived effective status invalid_output while its raw worker receipt remains unchanged. A low scientific score with G=1 is completed, not an execution failure.

Fallback may promote the already sealed reference package once per stage. Preserve original outputs/roles, complete input pools, former-incumbent diagnostics, source/frame, paper grid, challenger set, seeds and samples. Active roles come from the new `ComparisonPlan`. Keep the original source-stage name in sampling/binding identities; an outer rerun label is operational metadata. No fresh miner execution is permitted after reveal. An absent, failed or gate-invalid reference blocks fallback.

Create a common scientific generation under the new plan/binding and verify the whole active scored pool, including the reference. Reuse only identical eligible requests; candidate verdicts under changed packet/binding bytes are newly assessed. Rebuild pairs/audits/publication together. This one role transition is separate from the two source/frame correction revisions. Insufficient frozen budget blocks it.

### 6.4 Corrections, persistence and replay

A correction creates an immutable incrementing receipt with predecessor hash, reason, affected source/task/verdict references and evidence. Preserve original output bytes and paper seed. Source/task corrections reassess every affected pipeline; frame changes regenerate common samples using the same seed/ranking rule. At most two common target/frame correction revisions are allowed after initial sealing; unresolved defects at that bound hold the paper. Recompute every affected score and pair, including after a one-output verdict correction, and invalidate dependent audit/publication hashes. No pair-local Gold mutation or in-place overwrite is allowed. A pure score/audit recomputation may reuse unchanged source/verdict evidence, but creates a separately bound policy generation.

The initial scientific generation has a 1,800-second deadline from sealing the paper’s output pool. Recovery and corrections within that unfinished generation do not restart it. An authorized correction or baseline fallback after a generation has frozen receives a new 1,800-second deadline from durable activation of that common generation. Record its start, deadline, authorizing evidence and predecessor before dispatch. Preserve original packages, paper secrets, applicable attempt histories, the two source/frame correction limit and once-per-stage fallback limit. A hold, retry or elapsed deadline alone never authorizes a new generation.

Retain original sources/packages/CSVs, frames, samples, task cards, matching/search evidence, complete assertion packets, raw and validated model requests/responses, route/runtime metadata, usage, failures, verdicts, scores and revision lineage for the published receipt's lifetime. Persist each valid completed operation before releasing dependent work or cleaning its working copies. Recovery resumes outstanding prescribed work from durable receipts. Use Appendix B's exact serialization, hashes, numeric literals, pointer conventions and common bindings. Public diagnostics omit credentials, private gateway data and unrevealed tasks/secrets.

Record artifact dependencies by immutable input identities. Recompute only artifacts whose inputs or required bindings changed and all affected descendants; verify the complete common revision before freezing. Unchanged parsing, graph indexes and literal fragments remain reusable when their derivation inputs match. A task correction invalidates dependent judgments; a frame correction invalidates samples and dependent tasks. A changed global binding can invalidate scientifically unchanged requests. Apply §7.2's explicit replacement-only lineage exception; baseline-role fallback requires §6.3's new scientific generation. Every affected pipeline, score, pair and publication artifact must be updated together.

Cache reuse requires identical complete operation/role, model/runtime, parameters, prompts, response schema, source evidence, task, assertion packet, configuration and request bytes. Same paper, same numbers, renamed IDs or semantic equivalence alone is insufficient. Keep deterministic-derivative caching separate from model-response reuse. Cache technology and internal storage layout are implementation choices; these eligibility rules are not.

Before dispatch, atomically check the exact request identity. Reuse a valid completed response; if the same request is outstanding, attach the consumer to that operation; otherwise register one dispatch owner only if the logical operation’s persisted attempt budget permits another attempt; an exhausted operation retains its hold. Retain immutable raw requests/responses, validation outcome, provider request identity when available, attempt ordinals and the logical operation's shared retry history. Independent roles remain distinct requests. A technical failure is never its retry's answer; changed retry error context creates distinct request bytes within the same bounded attempt sequence.

Reserve each attempt durably before sending it. On restart, recover the same provider operation where the approved endpoint supports it; otherwise wait for the retained attempt timeout before treating an unresolvable outcome as a consumed technical failure. Do not launch a replacement while a recorded attempt remains live, or reset retry counts, evidence limits or the paper deadline. If the one permitted retry is exhausted, hold. A lost acknowledgement does not prove the provider performed no work or incurred no charge. Retain late responses as diagnostics; they cannot overwrite a terminal operation or bypass the paper's current common disposition.

Count each confirmed provider attempt and its available usage once, including failed/retried attempts. Record uncertain dispatch or billing explicitly; do not report it as a known zero-cost call. Cache hits and additional consumers reference that usage record without duplicating calls, tokens or cost. Record cache hits, shared consumers and unavailable billing separately. Retain actual source preparation, matching, primary, secondary, arbiter, expansion and retry workloads; sampled tile counts are not model-call counts.

Transparent input-prefix caching is permitted only through the approved route while preserving complete request content/order, response schema, model/runtime and frozen parameters. It caches input processing, not a distinct pipeline's verdict. Do not shorten or reorder evidence, add routes, or combine tasks/pipelines into one context to obtain cache hits. Record provider-reported cached-token usage when available.

Replay uses retained bytes and judgments without live provider calls. It verifies bindings, gates, source selections, task bits, exact fractions, score adapters, corrections and publication lineage. Temperature zero is not a substitute for recorded responses.

## 7. Integration and acceptance

### 7.1 Required interfaces and integration points

Implement the stated behavior within the repository's existing architecture. Private module layout, helper names, internal classes and choice of schema-validation library are flexible. Appendix B defines the required exchanged/persisted information and invariants; its Python realization is optional. Preserve all validation constraints and exact response contracts irrespective of implementation.

The integration target is repository commit `0ba3f4bada37fdef54a8845ca6ef1ee2596c023f`. Dispatch by protocol before parsing. Schema 1.1 uses `paper.id`; it must not enter a `paper.paper_id`/`logic.claims` parser. The following entry points must implement or receive the required behavior. The [pinned repository tree](https://github.com/DeSciClaims/Claims/tree/0ba3f4bada37fdef54a8845ca6ef1ee2596c023f) supplies their code context. Stored receipts likewise dispatch by their declared protocol with their own immutable runtime evidence. Require explicit dispatch at transport, projection, evaluation, scoring, audit and publication boundaries.

| Existing integration | Required observable behavior |
|---|---|
| `competition/worker_bundle.py::build_worker_bundle`; `competition/worker_guest.py`; `competition/worker_control.py::_run_once_locked`; `competition/worker_controller.py::run_claimed_job` | Deliver the complete common source/schema request; collect/hash actual JSON and CSVs safely before cleanup; persist execution/resource evidence; protocol-dispatch validation. |
| `validator/agent_v1/reference_client.py::ReferenceMinerInput`; `validator/agent_v1/record_projection.py::project_agent_artifact` | Reference executor returns the same complete package format. Build source/object indexes from original schema 1.1 content; do not project through claim units. Remote reference support is an activation dependency. |
| `validator/agent_v1/orchestrator.py::run_paper_silver_pipeline`; `competition/pair_evaluator.py::evaluate_weekly_paper` | Wait for all outputs/failures on that paper, evaluate one common pool, reuse each pipeline's one Q in every pair, and return a typed protocol-specific result through `WeeklyPaperEvaluation`. |
| `competition/scoring.py::score_pairwise_paper`; `validator/agent_v1/silver_scoring.py` | Return exact G/C/T/P diagnostics and Q through the existing score interface; bypass shared-equivalence divisors and unit-count scores. |
| `competition/evaluation_policy.py::FrozenEvaluationPolicy`; `competition/validator_workflow.py`; `competition/silver_runtime.py::silver_runtime_config`; `validator/agent_v1/model_usage.py` | Freeze/hash all effective rules, source callbacks, prompts, formats, routes and rendering; reject mismatches; retain actual request usage. Claim-unit J1/J2 objects and ten-claim/eighty-case caps must not govern this protocol. Outer judge_id metadata cannot override its four frozen routes. |
| `competition/replay_runtime.py::ReplayRuntimeBackend`; `competition/round_evaluation.py`; `competition/stages.py`; `competition/policy.py`; `competition/final_selection.py`; `competition/budget.py` | Retrieve complete source/CSV/verdict evidence; preserve main-PDF source_sha256 separately from source-packet hash; enforce common grid, ranking and minima. Fund the measured frozen cost profile; never reduce samples silently. |
| `competition/failure_policy.py`; `competition/stage_controller.py::start_failed_incumbent_reference_rerun`; `competition/final_controller.py`; `competition/replay_controller.py`; `competition/replay_store.py`; `competition/replay_final_selection.py` | Count effective gate failures correctly; promote sealed reference through the common plan without new extraction. Adapt the `start_reference_rerun` backend contract accordingly. |
| `competition/copy_audit.py::audit_final_output_copies`; `competition/code_audit.py`; `competition/code_audit_setup.py`; `competition/audit_calibration.py`; `competition/pilot_controller_factory.py` | Versioned copy diagnostics always have is_copy=false; equality is not a veto. Code checks are exactly build, evaluation_context and cap_evasion. Remove fragmentation/count-ratio vetoes and dispatch before incompatible artifact/count parsing. |
| `competition/review_policy.py::uses_miner_review`; `competition/gold_controller.py`; `competition/gold_override.py` | Require winner_selection=silver_tiebreak and gold_refused=false. Reject unit-based miner Gold/per-pair overrides for these receipts; corrections use the common scientific revision. |
| `competition/weekly_automation.py::_publish_final`; `competition/final_controller.py::select_ready_final`; `competition/replay_final_audit.py::audit_replay_final`; `competition/replay_final_selection.py`; `competition/result_controller.py::publish_selected_round`; `competition/replay_publication.py` | Verify protocol-specific audits, current common paper revisions, compatible plans and pair-grid hashes; reject semantic-copy=true. A scientific score revision invalidates dependent audits/publication. Replay makes no judge call. |

Copy receipts use `claims_source_copy_diagnostic_v1`, `is_copy=false`, `semantic_copy_check="not_applicable"`, executable-package-hash equality, source-packet-hash equality, and the bound evidence hash. These diagnostics have no Q effect. Independent build/context/resource checks remain binding.

Preserve pair-row keys `paper_id`, `challenger_hotkey`, `baseline_hotkey`, `challenger_score`, `baseline_score`, `infrastructure_fault`, and `details_public`. SCORED rows convert the two exact fractions once and use `infrastructure_fault=false`; public details bind protocol, Silver hash/revision and component references. Pending HOLD returns no rows. Common exclusions use the placeholders specified in §5.3. Keep full score breakdowns, task bits, populations, ranks, findings and usage in retained evidence. Do not fabricate covered-unit counts. `WeeklyPaperEvaluation.pipeline` must distinguish the sampled receipt/status from incompatible claim-unit results; preserve its usage/resources interfaces.

### 7.2 Storage and stage-plan operations

The external storage/controller contract must provide idempotent hash-checked `put_blob(sha256,bytes)` and `get_blob(sha256)`; append-only `put_receipt(sha256,bytes)` and `get_receipt(sha256)`; and compare-and-set `advance_paper_revision(round_id,stage,paper_id,expected_parent_hash,new_hash)`. Reject stale-parent writes without overwriting. Implement and test the remote service/runner changes, not only local adapters.

Store byte-identical artifacts once by hash while retaining every logical receipt and binding. Check expected length/hash on retrieval across storage boundaries. Protect blobs reachable from any retained receipt; deduplication never merges scientific objects, aliases, requests or verdicts. Shared worker inputs remain immutable and read-only; no pipeline receives another pipeline’s mutable state.

Provide atomic `append_common_replacement(round_id,stage,exclusion_receipt,expected_sample_revision,next_precommitted_paper_id)` with all §5.3 checks. Preserve history and schedule the complete entrant set in the same transaction. Do not misuse a baseline-rerun operation that cannot change a released paper grid.

`ComparisonPlan` binds stage roles and an output-independent `SourceGridManifest` with initial paper IDs, committed order and append-only replacements. Active membership is all initial and replacement paper IDs minus all recorded excluded IDs. Late results for an excluded paper never restore it to the denominator. Plans are ready before pipeline outputs; each paper's Silver independently binds its own sealed pool. There is no stage-wide extraction barrier or deadline delay while other papers finish.

Dispatch independent ready work without idle serialization, using bounded queues and shared concurrency controls: at most 16 global model requests, four case workers per paper and four independent primary requests for a source task. Preserve task dependencies, request order within an operation and common-revision coherence. Four requests are four separate model contexts. Prioritize ready work for sealed papers by earliest unchanged evaluation deadline, then paper/task/output identity; source preparation uses remaining capacity. Queue time counts toward the active generation’s 1,800-second deadline under §6.4; recovery, caching and batching never restart that generation’s clock. Keep the 120-second request timeout. Do not use deferred bulk execution whose completion cannot meet these limits.

Initial plan/grid revisions are zero, without a parent or replacement history, and the fallback flag is false. Each subsequent plan binds its exact predecessor and increments the plan revision by one; protocol, round, stage and reference identity remain unchanged. Replacement increments the grid revision once, retains roles/fallback flag, and appends exactly one validated replacement entry. Fallback preserves the exact grid and challenger roles while selecting the sealed reference as baseline and setting its once-only flag. Plan and loaded-grid protocol/round/stage/revision must agree.

A replacement-only descendant with identical roles may retain an unaffected paper's ancestor-plan receipt after checking lineage, current active membership and unchanged source/policy/task/sample bindings. Do not rewrite that receipt or rejudge it. A baseline-role change requires §6.3's common new scientific generation. Publish only mutually compatible comparison-plan, component, exclusion and Silver generations.

### 7.3 Tests and activation

Appendix D contains the complete E01–E50 catalogue, complete package fixtures, expected outcomes and executable fixture helper. Run mechanical/scoring/routing/replay tests with scripted source/judge responses; live calls belong only to qualification. Require all 50 cases, all V01–V30 rules (including optional V28–V30 where applicable), every label/relation/annotation enum and property category, strict boundary validation, exact sampling/fractions, complete search, common correction/exclusion/fallback, durable transport and offline replay. Include adversarial duplicates, wrong attachments/provenance, source ambiguity, CSV line breaks, source-image/table-continuation cases and organization-preserving transformations. Internal implementation changes must not change these outcomes.

For a smoke run, generate the complete P005 package and its actual CSV, validate all referenced contracts and hashes, and evaluate it using scripted source/judge responses. Require Q=1, persist the complete frozen receipt, disable provider access, and replay it to the same exact fractions and pair rows. The fixture generator supplies inputs; the implementation must generate its own frozen receipt. Test harness paths and command names are implementation choices; the required inputs, assertions and offline outcome are fixed.

Activation requires all of the following; failure blocks activation without authorizing policy changes:

1. **Conformance:** all E01–E50 pass, authoritative schema hash matches, required response formats validate, exact scores/pairs replay, and every integration adapter has a round-trip fixture.
2. **Scientific qualification:** a frozen signed truth corpus supplied by the deployment owner, with at least 200 positive and 200 negative cases, all 28 labels/all 10 relations/all 9 annotation kinds, at least two positive/two negative cases per label/relation, at least 30 admissible organization pairs and 30 material relation/grouping errors. The engineer does not invent truth to fit model outputs.
3. **Accuracy:** negative PASS/all negatives ≤0.02; positive (FAIL+HOLD)/all positives ≤0.05; all HOLD/all cases ≤0.05; admissible pairs with both PASS/all admissible pairs ≥0.95. Retain every case in its denominator and report exact counts and Wilson 95% intervals. Apply these thresholds after full secondary/arbiter routing; unresolved known negatives remain visible in the HOLD rate.
4. **Operational pilot:** at least 50 complete papers through actual workers/approved routes, two distinct pipelines plus reference, with at least ten cases each of multi-table, supplementary, equation/complex-context, and scanned/image or continued/multi-panel papers; categories may overlap. Allow zero silent truncations, inconsistent comparison grids, prohibited copy/fragmentation penalties or raw-output mutations.
5. **Capacity:** measure preparation separately; at least 95% of eligible pilot papers finish their initial post-seal evaluation within 1,800 seconds. Report subsequent authorized correction/fallback generations separately and retain their original deadlines. Include all actual production work in the funding profile; identify qualification census costs separately. Report p50/p95 calls, tokens, billed cost, peak memory and primary/secondary/arbiter proportions for every stage. Unavailable billing cannot prove cost adequacy. Measured p95 paper/package costs plus 20% reserve must fund the frozen stage minima.
6. **Recovery/publication:** replay a full pilot grid with providers disabled; exercise task/frame corrections, worker/provider failures and stale writes; require complete common rescoring, correct holds and current audit/publication lineage.
7. **Sampling diagnostic:** produce §7.4’s census/resampling report, including unresolved cases and its separate cost accounting. No unmeasured precision or ranking-accuracy claim satisfies this requirement.

Operational credentials, approved model/runtime descriptors, endpoints, budget and signed qualification truth are explicit deployment inputs. Scientific rules, score formulas, sampling and failure outcomes are fixed by this specification.

### 7.4 Sampling calibration

Run this diagnostic after the timed operational pilot; do not add its work to ordinary competition evaluation. The production sample is fixed at six C regions, four T blocks and four occupied P buckets, with the stated census and unanchored rules. Batch Q remains the equal-paper mean of the complete per-paper formula, never a product of separately pooled components.

Before pilot execution, generate a separate 32-byte qualification secret K and commit `SHA256(frame("QUALIFICATION_P_CENSUS", pilot_manifest_sha256) + K)`, using §4.1's byte codec. The signed pilot manifest identifies two distinct scored pipelines and the complete pilot paper set. After outputs are sealed, rank eligible pilot papers by `HMAC-SHA256(K, frame("QUALIFICATION_P_CENSUS", pilot_manifest_sha256, paper_id))`, with UTF-8 paper-ID ties, and select ten. Record their source-category composition; fewer than ten eligible papers yields an incomplete diagnostic report, never a replacement chosen for favorable outcomes.

On those immutable outputs, assess every occupied P bucket using the ordinary evidence and independent-review routes, including the original paper secret for actual primary-PASS audits. Keep the mandatory unanchored decision and G=0 skip; a G=0 output counts as complete for paired-Q diagnostics with Q=0 and null P. Store census judgments separately from the published sample receipt; reuse prior judgments only when the complete request satisfies §6.4. Use the approved request/concurrency limits and a separate 7,200-second census deadline per paper from its diagnostic start; corrections do not reset that diagnostic deadline. At the bound, retain unresolved outcomes and mark that census incomplete. Diagnostic discoveries follow the ordinary common-correction rules; they do not authorize pipeline-specific score overrides, adaptive sample sizes, or resampling outside that procedure.

For each replicate i from 1 through 2,000, derive `K_i=HMAC-SHA256(K, frame("QUALIFICATION_P_RESAMPLE", str(i)))`. For each k in {2,4,6,10}, use K_i with the paper's §4.1 scope and P_BUCKET stream to select the first `min(k, occupied_count)` buckets. Share ranks across pipelines and sample sizes. On the common set of papers with complete paired census outcomes, recompute exact P and Q with recorded G/C/T, applicability and unanchored outcomes fixed. Compute census comparators with the same §6.2 formula and all occupied P buckets selected. An unanchored-forced zero remains zero; G=0 retains Q=0 and null P. Empty assertions retain P=1. Never impute an unresolved census bit.

Persist a diagnostic JSON report using Appendix B's serialization. Include commitments/seeds, selected IDs and category counts, complete/incomplete paired-paper counts, retained judgment/request hashes, and results for each k: equal-paper mean Q and paired gain for every replicate, their census comparators, mean absolute error, root mean square error, and the nearest-rank 95th percentile of absolute error. An order reversal satisfies `sampled_paired_gain * census_paired_gain < 0`; report its frequency and separately identify census gains of at least 1/50 (0.02) in magnitude. A zero census gain has a null reversal rate; report sampled ties separately. For each pipeline, also report errors of its equally weighted mean P over explicitly counted nonnull-P papers in the paired-complete set. Report census calls/tokens/cost separately from normal validation. With no complete paired census, numeric sampling-error metrics are null with reason INCOMPLETE_CENSUS; an empty nonnull-P population likewise has null P metrics. These diagnostics condition on the selected papers, recorded G/C/T and judge labels. They do not establish source truth, total-score accuracy or a confidence interval for a 50-paper batch. The report is required qualification evidence; it does not automatically change sampling, ranking or acceptance thresholds.

## Appendix A. Policy configuration

These values are fixed for `claims-source-task-v1`. Freeze them before intake and bind the settings, implementation, execution envelope, parser, normalization, renderer, prompts, schema, and approved route descriptors into `policy_sha256`. They are not tuning choices for the implementer. A missing or incompatible deployment input is `CONFIG_INVALID`; a resource exhausted during an otherwise valid evaluation follows §5. Scientific weights and sample sizes cannot change during a round.

### Settings and execution envelope

`FrozenSettings` in Appendix B defines the exact configuration fields and types. The following table is the policy value reference. Limits not carried directly by that record belong in its hashed `execution_policy` or the identified route descriptor. Counts are integers; byte limits are decimal byte counts; durations are seconds.

| Setting or scope | Required value and interpretation |
|---|---|
| `protocol_id` | `claims-source-task-v1`. |
| Extraction schema | Version `1.1`; `extraction_schema_sha256=78f5b279dfbf4f2fec5fc83b2219350e43e3fece5c58e94f81fb5d146d10a5e2`. The complete schema is in Appendix F. |
| `integration_base_commit` | `0ba3f4bada37fdef54a8845ca6ef1ee2596c023f`. |
| `content_prose_tiles`, `content_table_tiles` | `3`, `3`; six content draws in total, with shortage/census handling in §4. |
| Prose/equation tile size | At most `400` whitespace-delimited words, using the sentence and ownership rules in §2. |
| `archive_blocks`, `archive_block_data_rows` | `4`, `5`; source tables then their disjoint data-row blocks are sampled as specified in §4. |
| `output_audit_buckets` | `4` occupied source buckets per scored pipeline, or their census when fewer exist; a nonempty `UNANCHORED` bucket is additional and mandatory. |
| Qualification sampling diagnostic | `10` pilot papers, the `2` distinct scored pipelines named in the signed pilot manifest, `2000` resamples at k=`2,4,6,10`, and a separate `7200`-second census deadline per paper (§7.4). Retain these values in the hashed qualification policy; they do not alter production samples or deadlines. |
| Content stratum weights | `1/2` prose/equation and `1/2` table when both are applicable; `1` for the sole applicable stratum. Shortages do not change these weights. |
| `content_weight`, `archive_weight` | `7`, `2`; renormalize by common source applicability only (§6). |
| `pass_audit_numerator`, `pass_audit_denominator` | `1`, `10`; exact seeded primary-PASS audit selection (§4). |
| `initial_lexical_matches`, `exhaustive_scan_batch_size` | `12`, `32`; include all exact hits, and complete the required fallback before certifying absence. |
| `embeddings_enabled` | `false`. |
| `source_token_budgets`, `maximum_evidence_expansions` | `[32000,32000,32000]`, `2`; one shared evidence-expansion allowance across drafting, review, and arbitration of the source request. |
| Source-table-layout calls | At most `8000` source tokens per generation, review, or reconciliation request, including mandatory context. Subdivide owned scope after evidence retrieval as needed. |
| `max_table_layout_split_depth` | `8`; deterministic region subdivision is separate from provider-error retry. |
| `structured_packet_tokens_per_shard` | `32000`; preserve joint dependencies and all original assertions (§4). |
| `technical_retries` | `1` per technical failure sequence, using the same immutable request payload; then common hold. |
| Model call parameters | `temperature=0`, `top_p=1`, `n=1`, `max_output_tokens=16384`, `request_timeout_seconds=120`, `tools=[]`, `tool_choice="none"`. Unsupported fixed parameters block configuration. |
| `primary_independent_requests_per_batch` | `4` concurrent primary requests for a source task; one pipeline per request. |
| `max_global_inflight_requests`, `max_case_workers` | `16` global requests, `4` case workers per paper, with bounded backpressure. |
| `sealed_evaluation_timeout_seconds` | `1800` per scientific generation: the initial generation starts at sealing that paper's output pool; an authorized post-freeze correction/fallback starts at durable activation under §6.4. Recovery never resets an active deadline. No stage-wide output barrier; source preparation is measured separately. Expiration produces common `HOLD_EVALUATOR`. |
| `maximum_common_corrections` | `2` source-frame/task correction revisions after initial sealing; unresolved work then holds the common paper. |
| `replacement_limit_divisor`, `minimum_replacement_limit` | `20`, `1`; post-selection common replacement cap is `max(1,floor(required_eligible_stage_papers/20))`. |
| `installation_timeout_seconds`, `extraction_timeout_seconds` | `600`, `1800` per pipeline-paper execution; stop guest processes before collection. |
| `memory_limit_bytes` | `17179869184` per admitted worker job. |
| `max_extraction_json_bytes` | `10000000`. |
| `max_csv_bytes_each` | `50000000`. |
| `max_output_bytes_total` | `210000000` for collected JSON plus CSV bytes; check archive framing separately. |
| `max_output_files` | `2048`, including `output.json`; at most `2047` CSVs. |
| `max_objects`, `max_relationships` | `200000` physical object definitions, `400000` relationship records. |
| Input bundle | `210000000` bytes; preflight before execution. An oversized common source bundle makes the paper ineligible for this worker profile. |
| Logs | At most `10000000` bytes, retained separately from extraction evidence. |
| Guest network | Only the frozen worker gateway; no access to other outputs, evaluator tasks or prompts, judge credentials, or sampling secrets. |
| Extraction transfer retry | One retry only for a proven controller transfer failure, with identical package/source/policy; keep both receipts and use the first successful complete result. Scientific or miner execution failures do not authorize retries. |
| `winner_selection`, `gold_correction_scope` | `"silver_tiebreak"`, `"COMPLETE_INPUT_POOL"`. |
| `output_copy_policy`, `is_copy` | `"DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY"`, `false`. |
| `code_audit_checks` | Exactly `["build","evaluation_context","cap_evasion"]`. |
| `organization_score`, `canonical_organization` | Both `false`. |

### Approved model routes

The deployment owner supplies exactly four route descriptors: `SOURCE_READER`, `PRIMARY`, `SECONDARY`, and `ARBITER`. Each specifies provider, credential-free API base, exact model ID, runtime/revision identity, context window, strict-response-schema capability, image capability, fixed tokenizer ID and implementation hash, provider parameters, and any approved reasoning-effort setting. Credentials remain outside hashes and receipts. No missing value authorizes an engineering-selected model or fallback.

`PRIMARY`, `SECONDARY`, and `ARBITER` must have distinct `(provider,model,runtime_revision)` identities. `SOURCE_READER` may share `PRIMARY` but must differ from `SECONDARY` and `ARBITER`. Appendix C maps each operation to its route and response contract. Freeze the exact returned model identifier and metadata when the provider cannot supply an immutable revision; replay then guarantees stored-response reproducibility rather than live regeneration of that response.

Preflight every used route for strict response contracts, all fixed provider parameters, image handling when any required evidence uses images, and enough input space for the largest source bundle, assertion shard, instructions/schema, and maximum output allowance. Use its pinned tokenizer to count requests. Never strip images, omit unsupported parameters, or silently choose another model. Record provider-returned usage; unavailable billing data remains explicitly unavailable.

### Other deployment inputs

Supply committed source and pipeline packages, assigned paper/extraction IDs, the immutable entrant roles and precommitted paper order, backend endpoints, signed competition budget/policy, and the independently signed qualification corpus required by §7. Backend storage, private module layout, and internal object representation are implementation choices; the observable contracts, integrity checks, cache eligibility, scheduling transitions, and replay behavior in this guide are required.

## Appendix B. Required interfaces and retained evidence

### Contract families

Use these six logical contract families at the stated boundaries. Nested records do not require independent classes, modules, files, or lifecycles. The reference declarations below define exact field names, required presence, types, permitted values, constraints, and validation predicates. Those interface obligations are mandatory; copying the Python module or using Pydantic is optional. An implementation using dictionaries, dataclasses, another language, or another validation library must accept and reject the same messages.

| Contract family | Principal records | Required boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Source and submitted packages | `SourcePacket`, `SourceFrame`, `SubmittedPackage` | Frozen actual files, source/version identity, complete coverage, provenance, original output bytes, trusted execution receipts. Anchors, source rows/columns, document bindings and file references are nested definitions. |
| Configuration and sampling | `FrozenSettings`, `EvaluationBinding`, `EvaluateRunManifest`, `SampleManifest` | Approved policy/routes, complete per-paper binding, hidden seed commitment, populations, exact selections, weights and replay traces. Ranking/trace steps may be nested. |
| Tasks and reconstructed assertions | `TaskCard`, `AssertionPacket` | Required scientific information, original JSON/CSV references, literal property values, scopes, relations, submitted/computed closures and complete evidence. Property/object/relation/boundary/CSV definitions may be nested. |
| Judge exchanges | Inventory, layout, card, review, match and judge request/response records | Strict operation-specific outcomes, required dimensions, source witnesses, exact inspected sets and bounded evidence requests. `RequirementMatch`, `SearchCoverage`, `DimensionCheck`, `Counterexample`, and error/request payloads may be nested. |
| Silver and scores | `ScientificVerdict`, `SampledSilverReceipt`, `PaperScoreVector`, correction records | Common source tasks, decisions on original output, complete retained evidence, exact fractions, null/zero distinctions and immutable revisions. |
| Competition integration | `CompetitionPairRow`, `PublicScoreComponents`, `CommonExclusionReceipt`, `SourceGridManifest`, `ComparisonPlan` | Common comparison grid, sanitized publication, score versus exclusion, role transitions, replacement order and revision lineage. |

Only `InventoryResponse`, `SourceTableLayoutResponse`, `SourceTaskBatch`, `SourceReviewResponse`, `MatchResponse`, and `JudgeResponse` are model-authored response schemas. The controller creates authoritative IDs, hashes, sealed tasks, receipts and scores. `CommandResult` is an optional command-line wrapper; no command-line interface or private module names are required. Its declaration specifies compatibility if that wrapper is used. Required evaluation, correction and replay behavior is independent of that wrapper.

### Serialization, identity, and coordinates

Every declared interface field is required even if its allowed value includes `null`. Reject undeclared keys, duplicate JSON keys, non-finite numbers, invalid UTF-8, and lone surrogate code points. Apply all conditional and cross-record checks as well as shape validation. Do not equate missing extraction properties with explicit null. Read only allowlisted artifacts and reject escaping symlinks.

Evaluator-owned JSON is hashed as UTF-8 `json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)` with no final LF, using JSON-compatible values and recorded array order. Retain those bytes and SHA-256. Original source files, extraction JSON, CSVs, worker receipts, prompts, and raw model responses are hashed over their actual bytes. Do not rewrite scientific inputs for hashing.

Parse scientific numbers as exact integers or `Decimal` with their original lexemes; a Boolean is not a number. `PropertyAssertion.value_json` contains the exact original UTF-8 JSON value literal, including numeric spelling and string quoting/escaping. Explicit null is the string `"null"`; a missing property has `present=false`, `value_json=null`, and `original_ref=null`. Preserve array order, object keys, scalar types and signs. These literal strings prevent the evaluator JSON hash codec from rounding scientific values.

An opaque `output_alias` identifies one immutable pipeline output. Keep the author/miner mapping outside model requests. One package can retain both Bronze and baseline roles. `SubmittedPackage.roles` records original roles permanently; `ComparisonPlan` selects active roles, including the prescribed promotion of an already sealed reference package. Include every competing package and the Bronze helper in the input-pool digest.

| Binding | Exact content |
|---|---|
| Runner `source_sha256` | Main PDF bytes. Preserve this meaning in worker, pool and replay adapters. |
| `source_packet_sha256` / runner `source_manifest_sha256` | Frozen complete source manifest including each main/supplement file hash. |
| `source_frame_sha256` | Trusted inventory, anchors, eligibility and tiling for one frame revision. |
| `input_pool_sha256` | Sorted submitted-package hashes and original role assignments for the complete sealed pool. |
| `policy_sha256` | Frozen settings and exact implementation, execution, prompt, schema, parser, normalization, renderer and route digests. |

Frame/card corrections retain source bytes. A change in supplied source bytes requires a new common source packet and fresh executions; it cannot be applied to outputs produced from different inputs.

| Coordinate | Required interpretation |
|---|---|
| `JsonRef.pointer` | RFC 6901 into the immutable original extraction for its alias; empty means root. Decode `~1` and `~0` once. Object IDs do not substitute for pointers. |
| Evaluator `CsvRef` | Header row `0`, data records `1..row_count`, zero-based column ordinal, exact delivered filename. A row is a parsed record, including embedded quoted line breaks. |
| Extraction `TableCellReference` | Data rows start at `1` and columns use declared keys. Header/caption quantities use named T references and source snippets; never fabricate extraction row `0`. |
| Trusted source row block | Zero-based data-row interval `[first_row,stop_row)`; header/note context is separate. Source-to-candidate row correspondence is independently checked. |
| Text and pages | Half-open Unicode code-point spans in frozen trusted text; internal `page_index` is zero-based; displayed source page is `page_index+1`. |
| Source rectangle | Normalized `(x0,y0,x1,y1)` with upper-left origin on the original page. Freeze parser settings and rendered evidence. |
| Source identity | A pipeline-local `Source.id` binds through `DocumentBinding` to verified source bytes/version. Matching IDs, titles or DOIs alone are insufficient; miners need not add a source hash to the extraction schema. |

### Cross-record validation

Apply these checks before consuming or freezing messages. An inconsistent evaluator message is an evaluator error. Assign a pipeline finding only when immutable source/worker/output evidence establishes a defect in that pipeline's submission. The scientific and scheduling procedures are in §§2–7 and Appendix C; this table specifies their interface consistency obligations.

| Interface | Required validation |
|---|---|
| Files and source packet | Every `FileRef` resolves under its declared artifact root with matching length/hash. Exactly one document is MAIN; document UIDs are unique; anchors bind to its actual bytes. READY requires every required document available and no blocking source error. `REFERENCED_SOURCE` denotes only an explicitly in-scope supplied cited source. Nullable version/URL never replace actual bytes. |
| Source frame | Unique/resolving anchors, tiles and table IDs; each owned region belongs to one document/stratum; ownership partitions included regions, with overlap only in context. Inventory accounts for every main/required-supplement region, including exclusions. Eligibility is source-defined. Reconcile every required companion mention before sealing; candidate omissions cannot establish table absence. |
| Source discovery and layouts | Validate all proposal keys, authorized geometry, source-supported continuation bindings and caller-owned part keys. READY layout responses have exact request/table/chunk/scope echoes and `complete_for_scope=true`; non-READY does not. Verify every accepted leaf, complete scope and disjoint row ownership under Appendix C. `SourceTable.data_row_count`, layout receipt ordered-row count and source table row-anchor count must agree and be positive for a qualifying numerical table. |
| Submitted package | COMPLETED means delivered, not scientifically valid. PACKAGE_FAILURE requires verified pipeline error; INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE requires trusted worker/evaluator error. Retain the execution receipt even when output files are malformed or absent. Aliases are unique; several roles may intentionally use one alias. |
| Evaluation binding | Cards, packets, verdicts, selections and scores share paper/round/stage, source packet/frame, full input pool, policy and revision. Stored package/task/packet hashes match. Artifacts referenced by a historical receipt are immutable. Repeated papers in different stages have distinct bindings. |
| Evaluation manifest | Verify every referenced file and all binding hashes. The private paper seed is exactly 32 bytes and matches the commitment. Its file never enters guest input. The approved comparison plan has the same protocol/round/stage; the paper is active and every active baseline/challenger/reference alias resolves in its sealed package pool. Evaluation waits for this paper's pool only and never executes or repairs a pipeline. |
| Original references and packets | Every alias/pointer/CSV coordinate resolves into the original output. Present `value_json` equals the exact original token sequence; missing values use the defined null envelope. All packet object/relation/property references resolve. Preserve material context, provenance, scientific sidecars, original closure and recomputation separately (§4). `packet_complete=false` or any omitted-due-to-limit reference prevents PASS, omission certification and frozen scoring. |
| Source tasks | Unique requirement IDs, valid supporting anchors, actual schema encoding constraints and no preferred object/family layout. READY content batches contain a real card; otherwise use INVENTORY_DEFECT. One archive card covers the selected block. The controller supplies permanent task IDs/hashes; OUTPUT_AUDIT cards are controller-authored. |
| Source review | Echo proposal kind/hash and validate proposal pointers and source anchors. Checked IDs are exact requested sets without duplicates for ACCEPT/REVISE; partial review records only actual work. ACCEPT/REVISE and corrected-artifact payload exclusivity follow Appendix C, including the CARD_BATCH/INVENTORY_DEFECT exception. No candidate content enters source-only review. |
| Matching | Requirement/output refs exist; retain every plausible contradictory match. MATCHED is correspondence only. Inspect exactly every component supplied in each successful scan request once. Stored available/inspected unique-ID manifests and counts agree; exhaustive fallback requires set equality. Inventory inspected-anchor sets likewise equal the requested sets and regions cover the inspected scope. OUTPUT_AUDIT supplies the entire occupied bucket. |
| Judge scope | Task/packet echoes match; anchors, original pointers and requirement IDs are supplied and resolve. CONTENT and OUTPUT_AUDIT require exactly the eight dimensions in Appendix C; ARCHIVE requires exactly its three. No missing, duplicate or extra dimension; REQUIRED_INFORMATION is NOT_APPLICABLE for OUTPUT_AUDIT. Unavailable evidence is never inapplicability. |
| Judge findings | Each failed dimension has a concrete source witness and original erroneous assertion, or a verified missing requirement with complete lookup. An omitted field has no fabricated pointer. SOURCE_SEPARATE/MUST_TOGETHER use only the family witness standard. Only complete ASSESSED responses yield task PASS/FAIL; other outcomes route through §5. |
| Evidence requests | Only typed bounded retrieval within the trusted scope; no external link following, arbitrary source addition, pipeline execution or submitted-output edits. Preserve each request and returned immutable evidence. Provider/parser faults are error receipts, not scientific counterexamples. |
| Sampling | All IDs resolve and selected IDs belong to their populations. Each selection trace includes conditional populations, parent table, requested count, rank digest and chosen IDs. Ranked unique-ID lists equal their populations; table streams list eligible blocks at top level and table/block draws in traces. Replay reproduces the exact order. C/T are common; P uses pipeline source buckets with UNANCHORED separate. Record PASS-audit selection independently. |
| Audit ownership | Every scientific assertion is assigned once using §4's provenance order and tie rule. Include scientific table metadata and sidecars; raw archive cell strings are T evidence. Recovering source ownership never edits an asserted quote/locator or removes an adjudicated provenance failure. Rebuild affected P sampling before freezing. |
| Scores and Silver | Enforce §6's exact applicability, gate/skipped-component and null/zero rules. FROZEN includes all eligible active scored aliases, resolved common C/T tasks for each gate-passing alias, all required P/audit decisions, consistent hashes and exact recomputation; no unresolved required state. A Bronze-only helper remains diagnostic unless it also has an active scored role. |
| Corrections | Predecessor matches the superseded receipt. Evidence is immutable source/review evidence; a source/frame proposal is complete and consistent with correction kind. Verdict corrections use decisive evidence and `proposed_artifact=null`. Never submit edited candidate output. Revalidate affected original outputs and rebuild the complete affected pair grid under §6. |
| Routes and parameters | Validate each provider-parameter file, approved endpoint, tokenizer ID/hash, context capacity, fixed parameters and required images under Appendix A. All route uniqueness and response-schema constraints in the reference validators apply. |
| Pair rows and exclusions | Validate exact active pair/alias membership, compatible Silver generation and public-component hashes. SCORED uses measured Q converted once; COMMON_EXCLUDED requires a terminal all-entrant exclusion and marked transport placeholders. Pending HOLD has no pair rows. Do not count excluded placeholders in any scientific denominator. |
| Plan and source-grid lineage | Verify predecessor bytes/hash before applying every `ComparisonPlan.validate_transition_from` and `SourceGridManifest.validate_transition_from` predicate, or its exact equivalent. Validate active membership, precommitted-order hash, unique next reserve, all-entrant exclusion, cap and atomic compare-and-swap. Replacement-only descendants may retain unchanged ancestor-bound paper receipts; fallback requires common re-evaluation under §6. No stage plan depends on unfinished paper outputs. |

### Error codes and stages

Use the following exact `ErrorRecord.code` values. Scientific counterexamples use the separate closed `CounterexampleCode` enum. `retryable=true` means an unused retry specifically permitted by the protocol remains; it never authorizes another scientific extraction. An unregistered error code blocks freezing.

| Category | Registered codes |
|---|---|
| PIPELINE | `PACKAGE_SCHEMA`, `OBJECT_IDENTITY`, `REFERENCE_UNRESOLVED`, `FAMILY_INTEGRITY`, `PARENT_PATH`, `ENUM_OR_CONDITIONAL`, `CLOSURE_INCONSISTENT`, `OBJECT_UNACCOUNTED`, `TABLE_PACKAGE_OR_COVERAGE`, `CSV_FORMAT_OR_DIMENSIONS`, `TABLE_REFERENCE`, `PACKAGE_MISSING`, `PACKAGE_TIMEOUT`, `PACKAGE_RESOURCE_LIMIT`, `PACKAGE_BUILD_FAILED`, `PACKAGE_SECURITY` |
| SOURCE | `SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE`, `SOURCE_VERSION_UNRESOLVED`, `SOURCE_UNREADABLE`, `SOURCE_INVENTORY_UNRESOLVED`, `SOURCE_TASK_UNRESOLVED`, `SOURCE_NO_TASKS`, `TARGET_DEFECT` |
| EVALUATOR | `CONFIG_INVALID`, `TRANSFER_FAILED`, `PERSISTENCE_FAILED`, `HASH_MISMATCH`, `RECEIPT_INCONSISTENT`, `LOOKUP_UNRESOLVED`, `EVIDENCE_LIMIT`, `PACKET_LIMIT`, `DEADLINE_EXCEEDED`, `CORRECTION_LIMIT`, `STAGE_REPLACEMENT_LIMIT`, `BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`, `FALLBACK_REFERENCE_UNAVAILABLE` |
| MODEL | `PROVIDER_ERROR`, `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT`, `RESPONSE_INVALID`, `RESPONSE_TRUNCATED` |

Stages are `CONFIGURATION`, `SOURCE_PREPARATION`, `EXECUTION`, `PACKAGE_COLLECTION`, `MECHANICAL`, `INDEXING`, `SAMPLING`, `TASK_BUILD`, `MATCHING`, `RENDERING`, `VERIFICATION`, `SILVER`, `SCORING`, `EXCLUSION`, `FALLBACK`, `PUBLICATION`, and `REPLAY`. Map provider errors to MODEL codes while retaining raw payloads privately. Map worker build, memory, timeout, security and missing-output statuses to the corresponding PIPELINE codes with original resource evidence. A controller hash mismatch is EVALUATOR unless immutable worker evidence proves the submitted manifest invalid. `INTERNAL_SCIENTIFIC_CONFLICT` is a diagnostic routed to scientific `CONTRADICTORY_ASSERTIONS`, not a transport error.

### Exact data definitions and optional Python implementation

The following reference uses Python 3.12 and Pydantic 2. Its declarations encode the required interfaces, including every `model_validator` predicate; generated JSON Schema alone does not express all of them. Implement those predicates and the cross-record checks above even when using another representation. With this reference, generate a model response schema using `ResponseType.model_json_schema()` and validate strict responses using `ResponseType.model_validate_json(raw_response, strict=True)`, after the duplicate-key/UTF-8/numeric checks. All declared constraints remain binding regardless of validation technology. The optional `CommandResult` wrapper does not make its listed commands or their module paths required.

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from fractions import Fraction
from math import gcd
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from pydantic import (
    BaseModel,
    BeforeValidator,
    ConfigDict,
    Field,
    StringConstraints,
    model_validator,
)


class Closed(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(
        extra="forbid", strict=True, frozen=True, allow_inf_nan=False
    )


Id = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(min_length=1)]
Sha256 = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(pattern=r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")]
Pointer = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(pattern=r"^(?:/(?:[^~/]|~[01])*)*$")]
Nonnegative = Annotated[int, Field(ge=0)]
Positive = Annotated[int, Field(gt=0)]
UnitFloat = Annotated[float, Field(ge=0, le=1)]
RuleId = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(pattern=r"^V(?:0[1-9]|[12][0-9]|30)$")]
Protocol = Literal["claims-source-task-v1"]
TaskKind = Literal["CONTENT", "ARCHIVE", "OUTPUT_AUDIT"]
Role = Literal["BRONZE", "BASELINE", "CHALLENGER"]
Bit = Annotated[int, Field(ge=0, le=1)]


def exact_int(value):
    if type(value) is not int:
        raise ValueError("an integer JSON token is required")
    return value


def exact_bool(value):
    if type(value) is not bool:
        raise ValueError("a Boolean JSON token is required")
    return value


class FileRef(Closed):
    relative_path: Id
    sha256: Sha256
    byte_length: Nonnegative
    media_type: Id

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def safe_path(self):
        p = self.relative_path
        if (
            p.startswith("/")
            or "\\" in p
            or "\x00" in p
            or any(x in ("", ".", "..") for x in p.split("/"))
        ):
            raise ValueError("relative_path must be a normalized safe relative path")
        return self


class JsonRef(Closed):
    kind: Literal["json"]
    output_alias: Id
    pointer: Pointer


class CsvRef(Closed):
    kind: Literal["csv"]
    output_alias: Id
    filename: Id
    row: Nonnegative
    column: Nonnegative

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def basename(self):
        if (
            "/" in self.filename
            or "\\" in self.filename
            or self.filename in (".", "..")
            or "\x00" in self.filename
        ):
            raise ValueError("filename must be a basename")
        return self


OutputRef = Annotated[JsonRef | CsvRef, Field(discriminator="kind")]


class ErrorRecord(Closed):
    category: Literal["PIPELINE", "EVALUATOR", "SOURCE", "MODEL"]
    code: Id
    stage: Id
    message: Id
    retryable: bool
    output_refs: list[OutputRef]


class SourceDocument(Closed):
    document_uid: Id
    role: Literal["MAIN", "SUPPLEMENT", "REFERENCED_SOURCE"]
    title: Id
    version: Id | None
    url: Id | None
    required: bool
    acquisition_status: Literal["SUPPLIED", "RETRIEVED", "UNAVAILABLE"]
    availability: Literal["AVAILABLE", "EXPECTED_MISSING"]
    file: FileRef | None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def availability_matches(self):
        if (self.availability == "AVAILABLE") != (self.file is not None):
            raise ValueError("availability/file mismatch")
        if (self.availability == "EXPECTED_MISSING") != (
            self.acquisition_status == "UNAVAILABLE"
        ):
            raise ValueError("acquisition/availability mismatch")
        return self


class SourcePacket(Closed):
    protocol_id: Protocol
    paper_id: Id
    round_id: Id
    state: Literal["READY", "HOLD"]
    documents: Annotated[list[SourceDocument], Field(min_length=1)]
    errors: list[ErrorRecord]


class SubmittedPackage(Closed):
    protocol_id: Protocol
    paper_id: Id
    round_id: Id
    output_alias: Id
    pipeline_digest: Sha256
    roles: Annotated[list[Role], Field(min_length=1)]
    source_packet_sha256: Sha256
    execution_receipt: FileRef
    state: Literal["COMPLETED", "PACKAGE_FAILURE", "INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE"]
    extraction_json: FileRef | None
    table_csv_files: list[FileRef]
    errors: list[ErrorRecord]

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def completed_has_artifact(self):
        if self.state == "COMPLETED" and self.extraction_json is None:
            raise ValueError("completed package requires extraction_json")
        if len(set(self.roles)) != len(self.roles):
            raise ValueError("duplicate pipeline roles")
        return self


class PageLocator(Closed):
    kind: Literal["page"]
    page_index: Nonnegative
    bbox: tuple[UnitFloat, UnitFloat, UnitFloat, UnitFloat]

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def valid_bbox(self):
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = self.bbox
        if x0 >= x1 or y0 >= y1:
            raise ValueError("empty or reversed page rectangle")
        return self


class TextLocator(Closed):
    kind: Literal["text"]
    start: Nonnegative
    stop: Positive

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def nonempty(self):
        if self.stop <= self.start:
            raise ValueError("empty text range")
        return self


class TableLocator(Closed):
    kind: Literal["table"]
    table_uid: Id
    first_row: Nonnegative
    stop_row: Positive
    column_names: list[Id]

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def nonempty(self):
        if self.stop_row <= self.first_row:
            raise ValueError("empty table range")
        return self


SourceLocator = Annotated[
    PageLocator | TextLocator | TableLocator, Field(discriminator="kind")
]


class SourceAnchor(Closed):
    anchor_id: Id
    document_uid: Id
    source_sha256: Sha256
    locator: SourceLocator
    exact_text: str | None
    rendering: FileRef | None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def readable(self):
        if self.exact_text is None and self.rendering is None:
            raise ValueError("anchor needs trusted text or rendering")
        return self


class SourceTable(Closed):
    table_uid: Id
    document_uid: Id
    anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]
    numerical: bool
    data_row_count: Nonnegative
    layout_receipt: FileRef | None
    data_row_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    logical_column_names: list[Id]
    header_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    note_anchor_ids: list[Id]

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def verified_rows(self):
        if self.data_row_count != len(self.data_row_anchor_ids):
            raise ValueError("source row count must equal verified ordered row anchors")
        if self.numerical and (self.layout_receipt is None or self.data_row_count == 0):
            raise ValueError(
                "numerical source table requires a complete verified layout"
            )
        return self


class SourceTile(Closed):
    tile_id: Id
    document_uid: Id
    stratum: Literal["PROSE_OR_EQUATION", "TABLE"]
    eligible_content: bool
    owned_anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]
    context_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    table_uid: Id | None
    first_data_row: Nonnegative | None
    stop_data_row: Positive | None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def table_shape(self):
        coords = (self.table_uid, self.first_data_row, self.stop_data_row)
        if self.stratum == "TABLE":
            if (
                any(x is None for x in coords)
                or self.stop_data_row <= self.first_data_row
            ):
                raise ValueError("TABLE tile needs a nonempty table row range")
        elif any(x is not None for x in coords):
            raise ValueError("prose/equation tile cannot carry table coordinates")
        return self


class SourceFrame(Closed):
    protocol_id: Protocol

            x0, y0, x1, y1 = self.bbox
            if x0 >= x1 or y0 >= y1:
                raise ValueError("empty or reversed source region rectangle")

class SourcePartIdentity(Closed):

class SourceTableLayoutRequest(Closed):
    request_id: Id
    table_uid: Id
    document_uid: Id
    chunk_id: Id
    requested_scope_anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]
    context_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    part_manifest: Annotated[list[SourcePartIdentity], Field(min_length=1)]
    split_depth: Annotated[int, Field(ge=0, le=8)]


class SourceTableLayoutResponse(Closed):
    request_id: Id
    table_uid: Id
    chunk_id: Id
    requested_scope_anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]
    complete_for_scope: bool
    outcome: Literal["READY", "NEED_EVIDENCE", "SOURCE_UNRESOLVED"]
    inspected_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    layout: SourceTableLayout | None
    evidence_requests: list[EvidenceRequest]
    unresolved_reason: Id | None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def legal_route(self):
        if (self.outcome == "READY") != (self.layout is not None):
            raise ValueError("only READY has a complete table layout")
        if (self.outcome == "READY") != self.complete_for_scope:
            raise ValueError("READY requires a complete layout for the requested scope")
        if (self.outcome == "NEED_EVIDENCE") != bool(self.evidence_requests):
            raise ValueError("evidence requests only for NEED_EVIDENCE")
        if (self.outcome == "SOURCE_UNRESOLVED") != (
            self.unresolved_reason is not None
        ):
            raise ValueError("unresolved_reason route mismatch")
        return self

class SourceTaskDraft(Closed):
    scope_kind: Literal["SCIENTIFIC_RESULT", "SOURCE_CONTEXT"]
    local_key: Id
    task_kind: Literal["CONTENT", "ARCHIVE"]
    requirements: Annotated[list[SourceRequirement], Field(min_length=1)]
    evidence_anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]
    source_scope_note: Id


class SourceTaskBatch(Closed):
    tile_id: Id
    outcome: Literal["READY", "NEED_EVIDENCE", "SOURCE_UNRESOLVED", "INVENTORY_DEFECT"]
    cards: list[SourceTaskDraft]
    evidence_requests: list[EvidenceRequest]
    unresolved_reason: Id | None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def legal_route(self):
        if self.outcome == "READY":
            if (
                not self.cards
                or self.evidence_requests
                or self.unresolved_reason is not None
            ):
                raise ValueError("READY requires nonempty cards and no error/request")
        elif self.cards:
            raise ValueError("non-READY batch cannot contain authoritative cards")
        if (self.outcome == "NEED_EVIDENCE") != bool(self.evidence_requests):
            raise ValueError("evidence requests only for NEED_EVIDENCE")
        if (self.outcome in ("SOURCE_UNRESOLVED", "INVENTORY_DEFECT")) != (
            self.unresolved_reason is not None
        ):
            raise ValueError("reason required exactly for unresolved/defective source")
        return self


class TaskCard(Closed):
    scope_kind: Literal["SCIENTIFIC_RESULT", "SOURCE_CONTEXT"] = "SCIENTIFIC_RESULT"
    task_id: Id
    task_kind: TaskKind
    binding: EvaluationBinding
    tile_ids: list[Id]
    output_alias: Id | None
    source_bucket_id: Id | None
    requirements: list[SourceRequirement]
    evidence_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    source_scope_note: Id

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def requirements_match_kind(self):
        if (self.task_kind == "OUTPUT_AUDIT") != (len(self.requirements) == 0):
            raise ValueError("only OUTPUT_AUDIT uses an empty required-information set")
        if self.task_kind == "OUTPUT_AUDIT":
            if self.output_alias is None or self.source_bucket_id is None:
                raise ValueError("OUTPUT_AUDIT requires its pipeline and source bucket")
        elif (
            self.output_alias is not None
            or self.source_bucket_id is not None
            or not self.tile_ids
        ):
            raise ValueError("common source tasks require tiles and no pipeline owner")
        return self


class InventoryRegion(Closed):
    document_uid: Id
    anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]
    kind: Literal["PROSE_OR_EQUATION", "TABLE", "EXCLUDED"]
    has_extractable_science: bool
    table_uid: Id | None
    proposed_table_key: Id | None
    source_label: Id | None
    source_regions: list[SourceRegionSlice]
    reason: Id

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def discovery_identity(self):
        if self.kind == "TABLE":
            if not self.source_regions or (self.table_uid is None and self.proposed_table_key is None):
                raise ValueError(
                    "TABLE discovery requires regions and either a verified table UID or a new source-local key"
                )
        elif self.proposed_table_key is not None or self.table_uid is not None:
            raise ValueError("only TABLE regions identify proposed source tables")
        return self

    evidence_anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]

SourceProposal = Annotated[
    InventoryRevision | CardBatchRevision | TableLayoutRevision,
    Field(discriminator="kind"),
]


class SourceReviewIssue(Closed):
    code: Literal[
        "INVENTORY_OMISSION",
        "INVENTORY_FALSE_POSITIVE",
        "WRONG_TABLE_IDENTITY",
        "WRONG_TABLE_LAYOUT",
        "SOURCE_MISSTATEMENT",
        "MISSING_REQUIRED_INFORMATION",
        "INVENTED_ENCODING",
        "WRONG_SCOPE",
        "INCOMPLETE_COVERAGE",
        "COMPANION_MISSING",
    ]
    proposal_pointers: Annotated[list[Pointer], Field(min_length=1)]
    evidence_anchor_ids: Annotated[list[Id], Field(min_length=1)]
    description: Id


class SourceReviewResponse(Closed):
    request_id: Id
    proposal_kind: Literal["INVENTORY", "CARD_BATCH", "TABLE_LAYOUT"]
    proposal_hash: Sha256
    outcome: Literal["ACCEPT", "REVISE", "NEED_EVIDENCE", "SOURCE_UNRESOLVED"]
    checked_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    checked_item_ids: list[Id]
    issues: list[SourceReviewIssue]
    corrected_proposal: SourceProposal | None
    evidence_requests: list[EvidenceRequest]
    unresolved_reason: Id | None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def legal_route(self):
        if self.outcome == "REVISE":
            if not self.issues or self.corrected_proposal is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "REVISE needs concrete source issues and complete correction"
                )
            outcome = self.corrected_proposal.payload.outcome
            valid_correction = outcome == "READY" or (
                self.proposal_kind == "CARD_BATCH" and outcome == "INVENTORY_DEFECT"
            )
            if (
                self.corrected_proposal.kind != self.proposal_kind
                or not valid_correction
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "correction must be a complete READY proposal of the same kind"
                )
        elif self.issues or self.corrected_proposal is not None:
            raise ValueError("only REVISE carries issues and a corrected proposal")
        if (self.outcome == "NEED_EVIDENCE") != bool(self.evidence_requests):
            raise ValueError("evidence request route mismatch")
        if (self.outcome == "SOURCE_UNRESOLVED") != (
            self.unresolved_reason is not None
        ):
            raise ValueError("source unresolved route mismatch")
        return self


class RequirementMatch(Closed):
    requirement_id: Id
    output_refs: list[OutputRef]


class SearchCoverage(Closed):
    index_sha256: Sha256
    available_component_ids: FileRef
    inspected_component_ids: FileRef
    available_components: Nonnegative
    inspected_components: Nonnegative
    exhaustive_fallback_completed: bool

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def bounded(self):
        if self.inspected_components > self.available_components:
            raise ValueError("search count exceeds index size")
        if (
            self.exhaustive_fallback_completed
            and self.inspected_components != self.available_components
        ):
            raise ValueError("exhaustive search did not inspect complete index")
        return self


class IdentityDecision(Closed):
    disposition: Literal["KEEP", "DISTINCT_SOURCE_RESULT", "CONTEXT_ONLY"]
    distinction_dimension: Literal["OUTCOME", "ANALYSIS", "POPULATION", "TIME", "METHOD", "QUANTITY_ROLE"] | None
    separate_source_identity: str | None
    evidence_anchor_ids: list[Id]
    reason: Id

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def source_supported_exclusion(self):
        if self.disposition=="KEEP":
            if self.distinction_dimension is not None or self.separate_source_identity is not None:
                raise ValueError("KEEP cannot assert an exclusion")
        else:
            if not self.evidence_anchor_ids or not self.separate_source_identity:
                raise ValueError("An exclusion needs a positive source identity and witnesses")
            if (self.disposition=="DISTINCT_SOURCE_RESULT")!=(self.distinction_dimension is not None):
                raise ValueError("Only a distinct source result has a distinction dimension")
        return self


class IdentityReviewResponse(Closed):
    task_id: Id

    inspected_owner_ids: list[Id]

class MatchResult(Closed):
    task_id: Id
    output_alias: Id
    state: Literal["MATCHED", "ABSENT", "AMBIGUOUS", "LOOKUP_UNRESOLVED"]
    matches: list[RequirementMatch]
    candidate_refs: list[OutputRef]
    coverage: SearchCoverage
    explanation: Id
    identity_receipt: FileRef | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def absence_requires_search(self):
        if self.state == "ABSENT" and (
            self.candidate_refs or not self.coverage.exhaustive_fallback_completed
        ):
            raise ValueError("ABSENT needs empty candidates and exhaustive fallback")
        if self.state in ("MATCHED", "AMBIGUOUS") and not self.candidate_refs:
            raise ValueError("matched/ambiguous result needs original candidates")
        return self
```
